title: "Railway API reference"
sidebarTitle: "API"
description: "API reference for the Railway plugin in Corsair: every `railway.api.*` operation with its input and output types, taken from the plugin's Zod schemas."
---

<Info>
**New to Corsair?** See [API access](/concepts/api), [authentication](/concepts/auth), and [error handling](/concepts/error-handling).
</Info>

## Access Group Members

### add

`accessGroupMembers.add`

Add a workspace member to an access group

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.accessGroupMembers.add({
	input: { accessGroupId: 'example', userId: '00000009-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  accessGroupId: string,
  userId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accessGroup` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `accessGroupId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `user` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `userId` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="accessGroup full type">

```ts
{
  createdAt?: string | null,
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  role?: string | null,
  source?: string | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null,
  workspaceId?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="user full type">

```ts
{
  avatar?: string | null,
  email?: string | null,
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  username?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### remove

`accessGroupMembers.remove`

Remove a member from an access group

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.accessGroupMembers.remove({
	input: { accessGroupId: 'example', userId: '00000009-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  accessGroupId: string,
  userId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `boolean`

## Access Group Projects

### attach

`accessGroupProjects.attach`

Attach a project to an access group

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.accessGroupProjects.attach({
	input: { accessGroupId: 'example', projectId: '00000002-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  accessGroupId: string,
  projectId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accessGroup` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `accessGroupId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `project` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="accessGroup full type">

```ts
{
  createdAt?: string | null,
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  role?: string | null,
  source?: string | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null,
  workspaceId?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="project full type">

```ts
{
  baseEnvironmentId?: string | null,
  botPrEnvironments?: boolean | null,
  clearanceDefaultForNewEnvironments?: boolean | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  deletedAt?: string | null,
  description?: string | null,
  expiredAt?: string | null,
  featureFlags?: string[] | null,
  focusedPrEnvironments?: boolean | null,
  id?: string | null,
  isPublic?: boolean | null,
  isTempProject?: boolean | null,
  name?: string | null,
  prDeploys?: boolean | null,
  primaryEnvironmentId?: string | null,
  subscriptionPlanLimit?: any | null,
  subscriptionType?: string | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null,
  viewerRole?: string | null,
  workspaceId?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### detach

`accessGroupProjects.detach`

Detach a project from an access group

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.accessGroupProjects.detach({
	input: { accessGroupId: 'example', projectId: '00000002-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  accessGroupId: string,
  projectId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `boolean`

## Access Groups

### create

`accessGroups.create`

Create an access group in a workspace

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.accessGroups.create({
	input: { name: 'api', role: 'MEMBER', workspaceId: '00000006-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  name: string,
  role: ADMIN | MEMBER | VIEWER,
  workspaceId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `role` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `source` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `workspace` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `workspaceId` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="workspace full type">

```ts
{
  adoptionLevel?: number | null,
  avatar?: string | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  discordRole?: string | null,
  has2FAEnforcement?: boolean | null,
  hasAutomaticDiagnosis?: boolean | null,
  hasGuardrailsAccess?: boolean | null,
  hasHipaaBAA?: boolean | null,
  hasSAML?: boolean | null,
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  plan?: string | null,
  preferredRegion?: string | null,
  projectCount?: number | null,
  redactedDueTo2FAPending?: boolean | null,
  restrictProjectVisibilityToGroups?: boolean | null,
  slackChannelId?: string | null,
  subscriptionPlanLimit?: any | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null,
  usersWithout2FA?: string[] | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### delete

`accessGroups.delete`

Delete an access group

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.accessGroups.delete({ id: '00000001-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `boolean`


---

| `role` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `source` | `string \| null` | No | — |

  createdAt?: string | null,

  has2FAEnforcement?: boolean | null,

  name?: string | null,

## Agent Usage

### get

`agentUsage.get`

Get unified AI usage for a workspace

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.agentUsage.get({ workspaceId: '00000006-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `workspaceId` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `billingPeriodEnd` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `hardLimitCents` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `hasCustomHardLimit` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `softLimitCents` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `totalUsedCents` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `usageRemaining` | `number \| null` | No | — |



---

### setLimit

`agentUsage.setLimit`

Set agent usage limit for a workspace

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.agentUsage.setLimit({
	input: { hardLimitCents: 1, workspaceId: '00000006-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  hardLimitCents: number,
  softLimitCents?: number,
  workspaceId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `boolean`


---

## Api Tokens

### create

`apiTokens.create`

Creates a new API token

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.apiTokens.create({
	input: { name: 'api' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  name: string,
  workspaceId?: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `string`


---

### current

`apiTokens.current`

Introspect the current API token and its accessible workspaces

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.apiTokens.current({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `workspaces` | `object[] \| null` | No | Workspaces this subject can operate on via this token or session. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="workspaces full type">

```ts
{
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### delete

`apiTokens.delete`

Deletes an API token

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.apiTokens.delete({ id: '00000001-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `boolean`


---

### list

`apiTokens.list`

Gets all API tokens for the authenticated user, one page at a time (first/after)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.apiTokens.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `after` | `string` | No | — |
| `before` | `string` | No | — |
| `first` | `number` | No | — |
| `last` | `number` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | The nodes of this page. |
| `pageInfo` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  createdAt?: string | null,
  creator?: {
    avatar?: string | null,
    email?: string | null,
    id?: string | null,
    name?: string | null,
    username?: string | null
  } | null,
  displayToken?: string | null,
  expiresAt?: string | null,
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  workspaceId?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="pageInfo full type">

```ts
{
  hasNextPage?: boolean | null,
  hasPreviousPage?: boolean | null,
  startCursor?: string | null,
  endCursor?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

## Audit Logs

### eventTypes

`auditLogs.eventTypes`

Get a list of all audit log event types and their description

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.auditLogs.eventTypes({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  description?: string | null,
  eventType?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### get

`auditLogs.get`

Get an audit log by ID

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.auditLogs.get({ id: '00000001-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', workspaceId: '00000006-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the audit log entry |
| `workspaceId` | `string` | Yes | The ID of the workspace |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `context` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `environment` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `environmentId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `eventType` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `payload` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `project` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `workspaceId` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="environment full type">

```ts
{
  canAccess?: boolean | null,
  canvasGroupRefs?: any | null,
  clearanceCachingTurnedOff?: string[] | null,
  clearanceDefault?: boolean | null,
  clearanceDefaultUpdatedAt?: string | null,
  clearancePropagatedAt?: string | null,
  clearancePropagationError?: string | null,
  config?: any | null,
  configEtag?: string | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  deletedAt?: string | null,
  iacPartials?: any | null,
  id?: string | null,
  isEphemeral?: boolean | null,
  name?: string | null,
  projectId?: string | null,
  sourceEnvironmentName?: string | null,
  unmergedChangesCount?: number | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="project full type">

```ts
{
  baseEnvironmentId?: string | null,
  botPrEnvironments?: boolean | null,
  clearanceDefaultForNewEnvironments?: boolean | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  deletedAt?: string | null,
  description?: string | null,
  expiredAt?: string | null,
  featureFlags?: string[] | null,

  id?: string | null,

## Billing

### createFreePlanSubscription

`billing.createFreePlanSubscription`

Create a free plan subscription for a customer

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.billing.createFreePlanSubscription({ id: '00000001-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `boolean`


---

### togglePayoutsToCredits

`billing.togglePayoutsToCredits`

Toggle whether a customer is automatically withdrawing to credits

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.billing.togglePayoutsToCredits({
	customerId: 'example',
	input: { isWithdrawingToCredits: true },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `customerId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  isWithdrawingToCredits: boolean
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `boolean`

## Bot Scope Bindings

### list

`botScopeBindings.list`

Railway agent chat bindings (Slack/Discord scope → workspace) across the caller's workspaces

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.botScopeBindings.list({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  canRemove?: boolean | null,
  id?: string | null,
  platform?: string | null,
  scopeId?: string | null,
  scopeName?: string | null,
  scopeType?: string | null,
  workspaceId?: string | null,
  workspaceName?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### remove

`botScopeBindings.remove`

Remove a Railway agent chat binding. Requires admin on the binding's workspace

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.botScopeBindings.remove({ id: '00000001-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `boolean`

});

## Clearance Tokens

### create

`clearanceTokens.create`

Creates a Railway Authentication token for a project's protected services and returns it once. Requires a workspace admin and the workspace's Railway Authentication entitlement

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.clearanceTokens.create({
	input: { name: 'api', projectId: '00000002-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

  environmentId?: string,
  expiresInDays?: number,
  name: string,
  projectId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `clearanceToken` | `object \| null` | No | A Railway Authentication machine token: gets non-human callers past the sign-in on a project's protected services. The token itself is only returned when it's created. |
| `token` | `string \| null` | No | The token. Shown this once; it isn't stored. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="clearanceToken full type">

```ts
{
  createdAt?: string | null,
  environmentId?: string | null,
  environmentName?: string | null,
  expiresAt?: string | null,
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Cloud Agents

### archive

`cloudAgents.archive`

Archive a cloud agent, hiding it from lists of unarchived agents. Requires permission to manage the agent

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.cloudAgents.archive({ id: '00000001-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `boolean`


---

### create

`cloudAgents.create`

Create a cloud agent

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.cloudAgents.create({
	input: { environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  cloudAgentCheckpointId?: string,
  codeEndpoint?: {
    port?: number
  },
  environmentId: string,
  name?: string,
  region?: string,
  source?: {
    branch?: string,
    repo?: string,
    serviceId?: string
  },
  variables?: any
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agentWsUrl` | `string \| null` | No | WebSocket endpoint of the in-VM harness gate, for the dashboard chat. Survives sleep: the domain is stable across sleep and wake. Prefers the machine observation; when that carries no gate domain it falls back to the domain network-cp actively serves, so an agent whose observation lagged still offers chat. Null on agents created before the gate existed, or asleep/absent machines. |
| `archivedAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the agent was archived; null if it isn't. |
| `consoleTargetId` | `string \| null` | No | Target ID for console or command execution. Null while unavailable. |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `domain` | `string \| null` | No | The agent machine's public HTTP domain (port 8080) — the live preview. Prefers the observation (falling back to the first declared domain when 8080 was never requested); when the observation carries no domains it falls back to the domain network-cp actively serves on 8080, so a preview shows even when the observation lagged. Returns null while the machine sleeps. |
| `domains` | `object[] \| null` | No | Every public domain on the agent's machine, one per port, in the order the machine declared them. |
| `environmentId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `previewDomain` | `string \| null` | No | The agent machine's own public HTTP domain (port 8080), known whether or not the machine is up: the observed one, else derived from the machine's id as the orchestrator names it. A fork's is its own machine's, never the source's. Null without a machine. |
| `privateDomain` | `string \| null` | No | Hostname on the environment's private network, e.g. &lt;name&gt;.railway.internal, reachable on any port from services in that environment. Null when the VM is isolated or its private endpoint isn't registered yet. |
| `project` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `region` | `string \| null` | No | Region the agent's machine runs in. Null briefly after creation, before the machine has been placed. |
| `sessions` | `object[] \| null` | No | Coding-agent sessions, one entry per session, newest first. Kept while the agent sleeps, so it still lists what it was doing. |
| `source` | `object \| null` | No | Workspace provenance: the GitHub repo cloned into the agent's workspace at create time. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | The current lifecycle state of a cloud agent. |
| `workStatus` | `object \| null` | No | Where work stands, computed on read from the native harness's run events, else the VM's session report. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="domains full type">

```ts
{
  domain?: string | null,
  port?: number | null,
  prefix?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="project full type">

```ts
{
  baseEnvironmentId?: string | null,
  botPrEnvironments?: boolean | null,
  clearanceDefaultForNewEnvironments?: boolean | null,
  createdAt?: string | null,
  deletedAt?: string | null,
  description?: string | null,
  expiredAt?: string | null,
  featureFlags?: string[] | null,
  focusedPrEnvironments?: boolean | null,
  id?: string | null,
  isPublic?: boolean | null,
  isTempProject?: boolean | null,
  name?: string | null,
  prDeploys?: boolean | null,
  primaryEnvironmentId?: string | null,
  subscriptionPlanLimit?: any | null,
  subscriptionType?: string | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null,
  viewerRole?: string | null,
  workspaceId?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="sessions full type">

```ts
{
  harness?: string | null,
  lastEventKind?: string | null,
  latestPrompt?: string | null,
  prompt?: string | null,
  sessionId?: string | null,
  sessionName?: string | null,
  state?: string | null,
  taskId?: string | null,
  terminal?: boolean | null,
  title?: string | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="source full type">

```ts
{
  branch?: string | null,
  dir?: string | null,
  repo?: string | null,
  serviceId?: string | null,
  status?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="workStatus full type">

```ts
{
  at?: string | null,
  kind?: string | null,
  message?: string | null,
  state?: string | null,
  unseen?: boolean | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

| `serviceId` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### fork

`cloudAgents.fork`

Duplicate a running cloud agent. With sessionId, the new agent keeps that chat and its history; without it, it starts with no chats

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.cloudAgents.fork({ id: '00000001-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `name` | `string` | No | — |
| `sessionId` | `string` | No | The harness's session id of the chat to carry over. |
| `variables` | `any` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `agentWsUrl` | `string \| null` | No | WebSocket endpoint of the in-VM harness gate, for the dashboard chat. Survives sleep: the domain is stable across sleep and wake. Prefers the machine observation; when that carries no gate domain it falls back to the domain network-cp actively serves, so an agent whose observation lagged still offers chat. Null on agents created before the gate existed, or asleep/absent machines. |
| `archivedAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the agent was archived; null if it isn't. |
| `consoleTargetId` | `string \| null` | No | Target ID for console or command execution. Null while unavailable. |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `domain` | `string \| null` | No | The agent machine's public HTTP domain (port 8080) — the live preview. Prefers the observation (falling back to the first declared domain when 8080 was never requested); when the observation carries no domains it falls back to the domain network-cp actively serves on 8080, so a preview shows even when the observation lagged. Returns null while the machine sleeps. |
| `domains` | `object[] \| null` | No | Every public domain on the agent's machine, one per port, in the order the machine declared them. |
| `environmentId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `previewDomain` | `string \| null` | No | The agent machine's own public HTTP domain (port 8080), known whether or not the machine is up: the observed one, else derived from the machine's id as the orchestrator names it. A fork's is its own machine's, never the source's. Null without a machine. |
| `privateDomain` | `string \| null` | No | Hostname on the environment's private network, e.g. &lt;name&gt;.railway.internal, reachable on any port from services in that environment. Null when the VM is isolated or its private endpoint isn't registered yet. |
| `project` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `region` | `string \| null` | No | Region the agent's machine runs in. Null briefly after creation, before the machine has been placed. |
| `sessions` | `object[] \| null` | No | Coding-agent sessions, one entry per session, newest first. Kept while the agent sleeps, so it still lists what it was doing. |
| `source` | `object \| null` | No | Workspace provenance: the GitHub repo cloned into the agent's workspace at create time. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | The current lifecycle state of a cloud agent. |
| `workStatus` | `object \| null` | No | Where work stands, computed on read from the native harness's run events, else the VM's session report. |

### get

`cloudAgents.get`

A cloud agent by ID, within an environment. Null when the agent belongs to another one, so a caller cannot render an agent it has switched away from

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.cloudAgents.get({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', id: '00000001-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });

Returns null while the machine sleeps. |

### list

### listMine

`cloudAgents.listMine`

Cloud agents you own, across every project and environment you can reach. Answers "where are my agents" in one request; `cloudAgents` needs one call per environment. Machine fields (`status`, `domain`, `domains`) read the last observed state in batched queries, so selecting them across many environments is fine

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.cloudAgents.listMine({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `archived` | `boolean` | No | true: only archived agents; false: only unarchived ones. By default both. |

### markSeen

`cloudAgents.markSeen`

Mark the agent's finished and failed sessions as seen, or only sessionId's. Requires permission to manage the agent

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.cloudAgents.markSeen({ cloudAgentId: '0000000c-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `cloudAgentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `sessionId` | `string` | No | The harness's session id. |



**Output:** `boolean`


---

### renameSession

`cloudAgents.renameSession`

Set a session's title, shown over its generated one. An empty title clears it

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.cloudAgents.renameSession({ cloudAgentId: '0000000c-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', sessionId: 'example', title: 'api' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `cloudAgentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `sessionId` | `string` | Yes | The harness's session id. |
| `title` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `boolean`

### sleep

Sleep a running cloud agent, keeping its volume. Processes on the machine are terminated; waking re-runs its entrypoint

## Cloud Agent Tasks

### cancel

`cloudAgentTasks.cancel`

Stop a task. Before its prompt is sent it never starts; after, only its own run is aborted. The task ends CANCELLED, with "The task was cancelled" as its error. A finished task is left as is

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.cloudAgentTasks.cancel({ taskId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `taskId` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `taskId` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### dispatch

`cloudAgentTasks.dispatch`

Start a task and return its handle. Creates a fresh agent by default; replyToTaskId continues the original conversation

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.cloudAgentTasks.dispatch({
	input: { environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', projectId: '00000002-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', prompt: 'example' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  agentName?: string,
  allowCreate?: boolean,
  cloudAgentId?: string,
  context?: any,
  conversationKey?: string,
  dashboardHandoff?: boolean,
  environmentId: string,
  externalRef?: string,
  idempotencyKey?: string,
  images?: {
    data?: string,
    filename?: string,
    mediaType?: string,
    url?: string
  }[],
  interactive?: boolean,
  metadata?: any,
  mode?: FOLLOW_UP | PROMPT | STEER,
  model?: string,
  outputSchema?: any,
  projectId: string,
  prompt: string,
  reasoningEffort?: string,
  replyToTaskId?: string,
  sessionId?: string,
  source?: {
    branch?: string,
    repo?: string,
    serviceId?: string
  },
  webhookProgress?: boolean,
  webhookSecret?: string,
  webhookUrl?: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `cloudAgentId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `dashboardRunUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Set when dashboardHandoff moved the prompt to the agent dashboard: the task is a new run there, not a turn on the agent asked for, and this is its link. Follow it there; its questions and approvals wait on the dashboard. |
| `deliveredAsReply` | `boolean \| null` | No | The prompt answered the agent's open question on this session instead of starting a turn; taskId is the task that asked. |
| `replayed` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `sessionId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `taskId` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### get

`cloudAgentTasks.get`

**Risk:** `read`

| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### respond

`cloudAgentTasks.respond`

Answer a question the task is waiting on. Wakes a sleeping VM to deliver it (the question survives sleep); poll cloudAgentTask for the outcome

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.cloudAgentTasks.respond({
	input: { action: 'ACCEPT', requestId: 'example', taskId: 'example' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  action: ACCEPT | ALLOW | APPROVE | CANCEL | DECLINE | DENY,
  answeredBy?: string,
  content?: any,
  requestId: string,
  taskId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `outcome` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `taskId` | `string \| null` | No | — |

## Databases

### cancelPitrHaWorkflow

`databases.cancelPitrHaWorkflow`

Cancels an in-progress PITR enable/disable rollout on an HA database cluster and resets its progress so the UI re-reads from the live cluster. No-ops when nothing is running

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.databases.cancelPitrHaWorkflow({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', rootServiceId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `rootServiceId` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `boolean`


---

### clearPitrHaWorkflowProgress

`databases.clearPitrHaWorkflowProgress`

Clears the recorded progress of a finished PITR enable/disable rollout so the Backups page starts fresh. A rollout still in progress cannot be cleared

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.databases.clearPitrHaWorkflowProgress({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', rootServiceId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `rootServiceId` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `boolean`


---

### disablePitrForHaCluster

`databases.disablePitrForHaCluster`

Disables point-in-time recovery on an HA database cluster with the same rolling rollout as enable. The backup bucket is left intact, so existing backup history is preserved

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.databases.disablePitrForHaCluster({
	input: { environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', projectId: '00000002-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', rootServiceId: 'example' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  environmentId: string,
  projectId: string,
  rootServiceId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `operationId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `workflowId` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### enablePitrForHaCluster

`databases.enablePitrForHaCluster`

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.databases.enablePitrForHaCluster({
	input: { environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', projectId: '00000002-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', rootServiceId: 'example' },

{

}

| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `workflowId` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### mysqlPitrRestorableWindow

`databases.mysqlPitrRestorableWindow`

The point-in-time window a MySQL service's binlog archive can restore to, resolved from the archive bucket: the oldest full backup (floor), the newest shipped binlog (ceiling) and every full backup's timestamp. archiveConfigured is false when the service carries no complete BINLOG_ARCHIVE_* contract

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.databases.mysqlPitrRestorableWindow({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', serviceId: '00000004-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `serviceId` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `archiveConfigured` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `ceilingAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `fetchedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `floorAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `fullBackupCount` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `fullBackupsTakenAt` | `string[] \| null` | No | — |
| `lineageCount` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `listingTruncated` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |

### pitrHaClusterReplicationHealth

`databases.pitrHaClusterReplicationHealth`

Live replication health for an HA database cluster (Postgres HA, MySQL HA). Use it to gate the Enable/Disable PITR buttons so a rollout can't start while a member is too far behind to rejoin. Returns null when the service isn't an HA root

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.databases.pitrHaClusterReplicationHealth({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', rootServiceId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `rootServiceId` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `allHealthy` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `checkedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `environmentId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `members` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `reachable` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `rootServiceId` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="members full type">

```ts
{
  healthy?: boolean | null,
  isLeader?: boolean | null,
  lagMb?: number | null,
  patroniName?: string | null,
  serviceId?: string | null,
  serviceName?: string | null,
  state?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### pitrHaWorkflowProgress

`databases.pitrHaWorkflowProgress`

One-shot read of the current progress of a PITR enable/disable rollout on an HA database cluster. Use it to rehydrate state on page load, then poll this field for updates. Returns null when no rollout has run recently

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.databases.pitrHaWorkflowProgress({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', rootServiceId: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `rootServiceId` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `clusterMutated` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `completedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `currentMemberServiceId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `direction` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `engine` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `environmentId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `errorMessage` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `failedAtPhase` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `members` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `newLeaderServiceId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `phase` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `rootServiceId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `startedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `workflowId` | `string \| null` | No | — |

  status?: string | null
}[] | null
```

### postgresHaClusterUptime

`databases.postgresHaClusterUptime`

One Postgres HA cluster's uptime SLI over the last N days: its row, its windows, its daily rollups. The cluster is named by any of its member services, since an environment can hold more than one. Errors when the service is not part of a Postgres HA cluster

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.databases.postgresHaClusterUptime({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', serviceId: '00000004-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `days` | `number` | No | — |
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `serviceId` | `string` | Yes | Any member service of the cluster. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `cluster` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `daily` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `windows` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="cluster full type">

```ts
{
  availability?: number | null,
  degradedMinutes?: number | null,
  downMinutes?: number | null,
  environmentId?: string | null,
  environmentName?: string | null,
  excludedMinutes?: number | null,
  lastRedundancyRound?: string | null,
  measuredMinutes?: number | null,
  platformDownMinutes?: number | null,
  projectId?: string | null,
  projectName?: string | null,
  recent?: string | null,
  redundancy?: number | null,
  redundancyMeasuredMinutes?: number | null,
  region?: string | null,
  rootServiceId?: string | null,
  rootServiceName?: string | null,
  unknownMinutes?: number | null,
  upMinutes?: number | null,
  windows?: number | null,
  workspaceName?: string | null
} | null

<Accordion title="daily full type">

```ts

### resetPassword

`databases.resetPassword`

Reset database credentials, coordinating all members for an HA cluster. On an HA cluster the rotation keeps running after this returns (`rotationInProgress`); follow `databasePasswordRotationProgress` until it reports DONE

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.databases.resetPassword({
	input: { databaseType: 'example', environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', serviceId: '00000004-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  databaseType: string,
  environmentId: string,
  serviceId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `newPassword` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `rotationInProgress` | `boolean \| null` | No | True when an HA cluster's rotation is still running: `newPassword` is live only once `databasePasswordRotationProgress` reports DONE, and `updatedVariables` is empty until then. |
| `updatedVariables` | `any \| null` | No | — |

## Dns Query Logs

### list

`dnsQueryLogs.list`

Fetch individual DNS query logs for an environment

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.dnsQueryLogs.list({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `afterDate` | `string` | No | Latest date to look for logs after the anchor |
| `afterLimit` | `number` | No | Limit logs returned after the anchor |
| `anchorDate` | `string` | No | Target date time to look for logs |
| `beforeDate` | `string` | No | Oldest date to look for logs before the anchor |
| `beforeLimit` | `number` | No | Limit logs returned before the anchor |
| `deploymentInstanceId` | `string` | No | Filter by deployment instance / sandbox ID (optional) |
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `filter` | `string` | No | Filter expression (e.g., @rcode:NXDOMAIN @qtype:A @domain:example.com @status:failed). Prefix a term with - to exclude it; repeat a key to match any of its values |
| `serviceId` | `string` | No | Filter by service ID (optional) |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  answers?: string[] | null,
  cnameChain?: string[] | null,
  deploymentId?: string | null,
  deploymentInstanceId?: string | null,
  qname?: string | null,
  qtype?: string | null,
  queriedAt?: string | null,
  queryZone?: string | null,
  rcode?: string | null,
  serviceId?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

## Environments

### applyChangeSet

`environments.applyChangeSet`

Experimental: applies an intent-level RailwayChangeSet and returns operation results

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.environments.applyChangeSet({
	environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17',
	input: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `baseConfigEtag` | `string` | No | Snapshot token from Environment.configEtag the plan was computed against. When set, the apply is rejected if the environment has changed since. |
| `commitMessage` | `string` | No | — |
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `input` | `object \| any[] \| string \| number \| boolean` | Yes | — |
| `waitForCompletion` | `boolean` | No | Defaults to true for compatibility. Async-capable clients set false and poll environmentChangeSetApply. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
} | any[] | string | number | boolean
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

| `changes` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `deploymentId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `diagnostics` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `operationId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `stagedPatchId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |

  path?: string | null,
  status?: string | null,
  summary?: string | null
}[] | null

### create

`environments.create`

Creates a new environment

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.environments.create({
	input: { name: 'api', projectId: '00000002-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  applyChangesInBackground?: boolean,
  ephemeral?: boolean,
  name: string,
  projectId: string,
  skipInitialDeploys?: boolean,
  sourceEnvironmentId?: string,
  stageInitialChanges?: boolean
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `canAccess` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `canvasGroupRefs` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `clearanceCachingTurnedOff` | `string[] \| null` | No | Services whose edge caching was turned off when the Railway Authentication default last turned on. |
| `clearanceDefault` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether Railway Authentication protects this environment's services that have no setting of their own. |
| `clearanceDefaultUpdatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `clearancePropagatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | When the current Railway Authentication default last finished reaching every service that inherits it. |
| `clearancePropagationError` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `clearanceServiceCounts` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `config` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `configEtag` | `string \| null` | No | Opaque snapshot token of the environment's IaC-relevant config. Echo it back as baseConfigEtag on environmentApplyChangeSet for optimistic concurrency. |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `deletedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `iacPartials` | `any \| null` | No | IaC resource address → partial name for this environment. '*' is the whole-project owner. Used so omit=delete only applies to resources this file already owns. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `isEphemeral` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `meta` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `projectId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `sourceEnvironment` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `sourceEnvironmentName` | `string \| null` | No | Name of the environment this one was copied from. A project token can read this on its own environment. It does not grant access to the source environment. |
| `unmergedChangesCount` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### iacPartialRelease

`environments.iacPartialRelease`

Release resources from a named IaC partial without deleting, redeploying, or changing them. Omit resources to release the entire partial. Whole-project management is possible once no named partials remain. A later named-partial apply can claim released resources again

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.environments.iacPartialRelease({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', partial: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `baseConfigEtag` | `string` | No | Snapshot token from Environment.configEtag the ownership preview was computed against. When set, the release is rejected if the environment has changed since. |
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `partial` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `resources` | `string[]` | No | Resource addresses to release (for example service.api). Every address must belong to the named partial. Omit to release all of its resources; an empty list is rejected. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `affectedResources` | `string[] \| null` | No | Resource addresses whose ownership changed. |
| `iacPartials` | `any \| null` | No | The environment's complete ownership map after the change. |



---

### iacPartialTransfer

`environments.iacPartialTransfer`

Transfer resources between named IaC partials without deleting, redeploying, or changing them. Omit resources to transfer the entire source partial. The destination may be a new or existing partial

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.environments.iacPartialTransfer({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', fromPartial: 'example', toPartial: 'example' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `baseConfigEtag` | `string` | No | Snapshot token from Environment.configEtag the ownership preview was computed against. When set, the transfer is rejected if the environment has changed since. |
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `fromPartial` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `resources` | `string[]` | No | Resource addresses to transfer (for example service.api). Every address must belong to fromPartial. Omit to transfer all of its resources; an empty list is rejected. |
| `toPartial` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `affectedResources` | `string[] \| null` | No | Resource addresses whose ownership changed. |
| `iacPartials` | `any \| null` | No | The environment's complete ownership map after the change. |



---

### logs

`environments.logs`

Fetch logs for a project environment. Build logs are excluded unless a snapshot ID is explicitly provided in the filter

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.environments.logs({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `afterDate` | `string` | No | Latest date to look for logs after the anchor |
| `afterLimit` | `number` | No | Limit the number of logs returned after the anchor |
| `anchorDate` | `string` | No | Target date time to look for logs |
| `beforeDate` | `string` | No | Oldest date to look for logs before the anchor |
| `beforeLimit` | `number` | No | Limit the number of logs returned before the anchor |
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `filter` | `string` | No | Filter logs using a query syntax |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  attributes?: {
    key?: string | null,
    value?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  message?: string | null,
  severity?: string | null,
  tags?: {
    deploymentId?: string | null,
    deploymentInstanceId?: string | null,
    environmentId?: string | null,
    projectId?: string | null,
    serviceId?: string | null,
    snapshotId?: string | null
  } | null,
  timestamp?: string | null
}[]
```

</AccordionGroup>

### updateClearanceDefault

`environments.updateClearanceDefault`

Sets whether Railway Authentication protects an environment's services that have no setting of their own, and pushes it to them. Requires a workspace admin; turning it on also requires the workspace's Railway Authentication entitlement. Services with edge caching on that it protects have their caching turned off

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.environments.updateClearanceDefault({
	input: { enabled: true, environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', projectId: '00000002-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  enabled: boolean,
  environmentId: string,
  projectId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `boolean`


---

### upsertConfigPlanComment

`environments.upsertConfigPlanComment`

Posts or updates a `railway config plan` comment on a GitHub pull request under the Railway app identity. The caller proves it runs in the target repo with a GitHub Actions OIDC token; the comment body is composed server-side from the structured plan

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.environments.upsertConfigPlanComment({
	input: {
		environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17',
		githubOidcToken: 'example',
		plan: 'example',
		prNumber: 1,
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  environmentId: string,
  githubOidcToken: string,
  plan: any,
  prNumber: number
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `commentId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `url` | `string \| null` | No | — |

## Http Metrics

### duration

`httpMetrics.duration`

Get HTTP request duration metrics for a service (avg, p50, p90, p95, p99)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.httpMetrics.duration({
	endDate: '2026-04-09T10:00:00Z',
	environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17',
	serviceId: '00000004-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17',
	startDate: '2026-04-09T10:00:00Z',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `endDate` | `string` | Yes | The end of the period to get metrics for. |
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `method` | `string` | No | Filter by HTTP method (e.g., GET, POST) |
| `path` | `string` | No | Filter by request path |
| `serviceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `startDate` | `string` | Yes | The start of the period to get metrics for. |
| `statusCode` | `number` | No | Filter by HTTP status code (e.g., 200, 404, 500) |
| `stepSeconds` | `number` | No | The frequency of data points in the response. If the `stepSeconds` is 60, then the response will contain one data point per minute. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `samples` | `object[] \| null` | No | The samples of HTTP duration metrics. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="samples full type">

```ts
{
  p50?: number | null,
  p90?: number | null,
  p95?: number | null,
  p99?: number | null,
  ts?: number | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

## Metrics

### get

`metrics.get`

Get metrics for a project, environment, and service

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.metrics.get({
	measurements: ['AGENT_CACHE_READ_TOKENS'],
	startDate: '2026-04-09T10:00:00Z',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `averagingWindowSeconds` | `number` | No | The averaging window when computing CPU usage. By default, it is the same as the `sampleRateSeconds`. |
| `endDate` | `string` | No | The end of the period to get metrics for. If not provided, the current datetime is used. |
| `environmentId` | `string` | No | — |
| `groupBy` | `(BILLABLE \| DEPLOYMENT_ID \| DEPLOYMENT_INSTANCE_ID \| ENVIRONMENT_ID \| FEATURE \| HOST_TYPE \| KEY_UNSPECIFIED \| MODEL \| PLUGIN_ID \| PROJECT_ID \| REGION \| SERVICE_ID \| UNRECOGNIZED \| VM_ID \| VOLUME_ID \| VOLUME_INSTANCE_ID \| WORKLOAD_KIND)[]` | No | What to group the aggregated usage by. By default, it is grouped over the entire project. |
| `includeDeleted` | `boolean` | No | Whether or not to include deleted projects in the results |
| `measurements` | `(AGENT_CACHE_READ_TOKENS \| AGENT_CACHE_WRITE_TOKENS \| AGENT_INPUT_TOKENS \| AGENT_OUTPUT_TOKENS \| AGENT_SPEND_USD \| BACKUP_USAGE_GB \| CPU_LIMIT \| CPU_USAGE \| CPU_USAGE_2 \| DISK_USAGE_GB \| EPHEMERAL_DISK_USAGE_GB \| MEASUREMENT_UNSPECIFIED \| MEMORY_LIMIT_GB \| MEMORY_USAGE_GB \| NETWORK_RX_GB \| NETWORK_TX_GB \| UNRECOGNIZED)[]` | Yes | — |
| `projectId` | `string` | No | — |
| `sampleRateSeconds` | `number` | No | The frequency of data points in the response. If the `sampleRateSeconds` is 60, then the response will contain one data point per minute. |
| `serviceId` | `string` | No | — |
| `startDate` | `string` | Yes | The start of the period to get metrics for. |
| `volumeId` | `string` | No | — |
| `volumeInstanceExternalId` | `string` | No | — |
| `workspaceId` | `string` | No | — |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  measurement?: string | null,
  tags?: {
    deploymentId?: string | null,
    deploymentInstanceId?: string | null,
    environmentId?: string | null,
    projectId?: string | null,
    region?: string | null,
    serviceId?: string | null,
    volumeId?: string | null,
    volumeInstanceId?: string | null
  } | null,
  values?: {
    ts?: number | null,
    value?: number | null
  }[] | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

| `anchorDate` | `string` | No | Target date time to look for logs |
| `beforeDate` | `string` | No | Oldest date to look for logs before the anchor |

| `filter` | `string` | No | Filter expression (e.g., @protocol:tcp @direction:egress @dropped:true) |

## Sandbox Checkpoints

### create

`sandboxCheckpoints.create`

Capture a running sandbox's current disk into a reusable, named checkpoint. Synchronous: the checkpoint is ready when this returns

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.sandboxCheckpoints.create({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', name: 'api', sandboxId: '0000000d-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|

| `name` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `sandboxId` | `string` | Yes | — |

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `environmentId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `key` | `string \| null` | No | The checkpoint's identity within the environment (same as id): a recipe content hash for a built template, or the user-given name for one captured from a sandbox. |

  environmentId: string,
  idleTimeoutMinutes?: number,
  networkIsolation?: ISOLATED | PRIVATE,
  publicDomains?: {
    port: number,
    prefix?: string
  }[],
  region?: string,
  resources?: {
    cpu?: number,
    memoryGB?: number
  },

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

| `idleTimeoutMinutes` | `number \| null` | No | Minutes of inactivity before the sandbox is destroyed. 0 means it never idles out; null means unknown, including for a destroyed sandbox. |
| `networkIsolation` | `string \| null` | No | Controls a sandbox's access to the environment's private network. |
| `privateDomain` | `string \| null` | No | Hostname on the environment's private network, e.g. &lt;name&gt;.railway.internal, reachable on any port from services in that environment. Null when the VM is isolated or its private endpoint isn't registered yet. |
| `region` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<Accordion title="domains full type">

{
  domain?: string | null,
  port?: number | null,
  prefix?: string | null

</AccordionGroup>

| `privateDomain` | `string \| null` | No | Hostname on the environment's private network, e.g. &lt;name&gt;.railway.internal, reachable on any port from services in that environment. Null when the VM is isolated or its private endpoint isn't registered yet. |
| `region` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="domains full type">

```ts
{
  domain?: string | null,
  port?: number | null,
  prefix?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### list

`sandboxes.list`

List sandboxes in an environment, newest first. Passing first/after paginates; omitting them returns the full list, one page at a time (first/after)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.sandboxes.list({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `active` | `boolean` | No | true = live sandboxes only (excludes destroyed; FAILED and transitional states are still included), false = destroyed only, omitted = all. |
| `after` | `string` | No | — |
| `before` | `string` | No | — |
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `first` | `number` | No | — |
| `last` | `number` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | The nodes of this page. |
| `pageInfo` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  createdAt?: string | null,
  domains?: {
    domain?: string | null,
    port?: number | null,
    prefix?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  environmentId?: string | null,
  id?: string | null,
  idleTimeoutMinutes?: number | null,
  networkIsolation?: string | null,
  privateDomain?: string | null,
  region?: string | null,
  status?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="pageInfo full type">

```ts
{
  hasNextPage?: boolean | null,
  hasPreviousPage?: boolean | null,
  startCursor?: string | null,
  endCursor?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### list

### clearAutoUpdateSnooze

`serviceInstances.clearAutoUpdateSnooze`

Undo an active auto-update skip for a service instance, applied immediately (no config staging, no redeploy). The recurring maintenance window and update policy are untouched — the next scheduled occurrence fires normally again

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.serviceInstances.clearAutoUpdateSnooze({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', serviceId: '00000004-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `serviceId` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `boolean`

|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `activeDeployments` | `object[] \| null` | No | All currently active (deployed and running) deployments for this service instance |
| `autoInstrumentationEnabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | Instrument the service's processes with eBPF (OBI) for supported runtimes, no code changes. Only active while tracingEnabled is true. |
| `buildCommand` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `builder` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `clearance` | `boolean \| null` | No | This service's own Railway Authentication setting. Null means no setting of its own: it follows the environment's default (see clearanceEffective). |
| `clearanceEffective` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether Railway Authentication protects this service's HTTP domains: its own setting, else its environment's default. |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `cronSchedule` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `deletedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `dockerfilePath` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `drainingSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `edgeConfig` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `environmentId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `hasEverDeployed` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether any deployment was ever created for this service instance, including deployments that have since been removed. Distinguishes a service that was torn down (all deployments removed) from one that has never deployed — latestDeployment and activeDeployments are null/empty for both. |
| `healthcheckPath` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `healthcheckTimeout` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `hideUnhealthyReplicas` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `ipv6EgressEnabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `isUpdatable` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `latestDeployment` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `nextCronRunAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `nixpacksPlan` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `numReplicas` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `overlapSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `preDeployCommand` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `preDeployTimeoutSeconds` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `railpackInfo` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `railwayConfigFile` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `region` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `resolvedFileConfig` | `object \| null` | No | The repo config file as of the most recent deployment that read it. |
| `restartPolicyMaxRetries` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `restartPolicyType` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `rootDirectory` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `service` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `serviceId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `serviceName` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `sleepApplication` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `source` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `startCommand` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### snoozeAutoUpdate

`serviceInstances.snoozeAutoUpdate`

Skip the next scheduled auto-update for a service instance. Takes effect immediately, with no staged change and no redeploy. The maintenance window and update policy stay as they are. This only delays the next scheduled update

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.serviceInstances.snoozeAutoUpdate({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', serviceId: '00000004-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `serviceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `snoozeDays` | `number` | No | How many days to skip, from now (default 7 — covers any weekly schedule shape). Between 1 and 14. |



**Output:** `boolean`

### updateAutoUpdateSchedule

`serviceInstances.updateAutoUpdateSchedule`

Update the auto-update maintenance window for a service instance, applied immediately (no config staging, no redeploy). Only the schedule changes — the update policy is untouched

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.serviceInstances.updateAutoUpdateSchedule({
	environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17',
	schedule: [{ day: 1, endHour: 1, startHour: 1 }],
	serviceId: '00000004-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `schedule` | `object[]` | Yes | The full replacement set of weekly UTC maintenance windows (at least one) |
| `serviceId` | `string` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="schedule full type">

```ts
{
  day: number,
  endHour: number,
  startHour: number
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `boolean`

### updateClearance

`serviceInstances.updateClearance`

Sets a service instance's own Railway Authentication setting: on, off, or none (follow the environment's default). Requires a workspace admin; ending up on also requires the workspace's Railway Authentication entitlement and edge caching off

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.serviceInstances.updateClearance({
	input: { environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', projectId: '00000002-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', serviceId: '00000004-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>

```ts
{
  enabled?: boolean,
  environmentId: string,
  projectId: string,
  serviceId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `boolean`

## Services

### acknowledgeTakedown

`services.acknowledgeTakedown`

Record that a workspace admin confirms subsequent deployments will exclude the content named in this notice, lifting its persistent takedown restriction. any other restriction on the service, and any other outstanding takedown, still applies. It permits a subsequent deployment; it does not restore an earlier one, and it is not Railway verifying the removal

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.services.acknowledgeTakedown({
	input: { acknowledgmentId: 'example', statement: 'example' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  acknowledgmentId: string,
  statement: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `acknowledgedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `acknowledgedBy` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `acknowledgedStatement` | `string \| null` | No | The attestation exactly as it was worded when it was confirmed. |
| `clearedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `customerNotice` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `hasExternalNotice` | `boolean \| null` | No | False when no customer-visible notice beyond the dashboard page was recorded, so the UI can say so rather than implying the original complaint is linked. |
| `headline` | `string \| null` | No | Customer-safe statement of what happened and why. |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `instruction` | `string \| null` | No | What the customer has to do before deploying again. |
| `isApplying` | `boolean \| null` | No | The service teardown has not yet completed; acknowledgment remains unavailable. |
| `noticeKind` | `string \| null` | No | Which complaint a takedown answered. OTHER keeps generic wording rather than asserting a legal basis Railway did not record. |
| `noticeUrl` | `string \| null` | No | Where to read the notice. Always resolvable: it falls back to the dashboard restriction detail page. |
| `restrictionId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `statement` | `string \| null` | No | The attestation the customer must explicitly confirm. Opening or reading the notice is not acknowledgment. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `takenDownAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="acknowledgedBy full type">

```ts
{
  avatar?: string | null,
  email?: string | null,
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  username?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### delete

`services.delete`

Deletes a service

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.services.delete({ id: '00000001-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `environmentId` | `string` | No | Environment ID. When set, the service is deleted only in that environment; the service itself is deleted once no instances remain in any environment. When omitted, the service is deleted in every environment that is not a fork of another environment. |
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `boolean`

### purgeCache

`services.purgeCache`

Purges the CDN cache for a service. Bumps the edge config's purge epoch so every edge node treats prior cached entries as stale on next request. Idempotent; returns true even if CDN is disabled for the service

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.services.purgeCache({
	input: { environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', scope: 'ALL', serviceId: '00000004-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  environmentId: string,
  scope: ALL | HTML,
  serviceId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `boolean`

### setUnderAttackMode

`services.setUnderAttackMode`

Enables or disables Under Attack Mode for a service. While enabled, the edge serves a browser challenge to unverified visitors of the service's edge-routed domains; non-browser clients (APIs, webhooks) without a clearance cookie are rejected with a 429. Optionally time-boxed via durationSeconds, after which the mode disarms automatically

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.services.setUnderAttackMode({
	input: { enabled: true, environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', serviceId: '00000004-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  durationSeconds?: number,
  enabled: boolean,
  environmentId: string,
  serviceId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `caching` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `edgeRules` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `overrides` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `purgeEpoch` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `purgeEpochByKind` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `tracing` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `underAttackModeUntil` | `number \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="caching full type">

```ts
{
  defaultTtlSeconds?: number | null,
  htmlCaching?: string | null,
  mode?: string | null,
  purgeOnDeploy?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="tracing full type">

```ts
{
  enabled?: boolean | null,
  sampleRate?: number | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### updateEdgeRules

`services.updateEdgeRules`

Replaces the service's edge rules (whole-section replace; null clears). Rules are boolean expressions over request attributes plus one action (block/allow/challenge/redirect/cache_override), evaluated at the edge before requests reach the service. The ruleset is validated server-side; invalid rulesets are rejected with per-rule diagnostics and nothing is written. Rules without an id get a server-assigned stable `rul_` id

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.services.updateEdgeRules({
	input: { environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', serviceId: '00000004-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  edgeRules?: any,
  environmentId: string,
  serviceId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `caching` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `edgeRules` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `enabled` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `overrides` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `purgeEpoch` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `purgeEpochByKind` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `tracing` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `underAttackModeUntil` | `number \| null` | No | — |

} | null
```

### updateKind

`services.updateKind`

Makes an ordinary service a Website (kind WEBSITE) and redeploys it in every environment where it has a deployment. A Website can't be made an ordinary service (kind null) yet. Returns false when the service already has that kind

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.services.updateKind({
	input: { serviceId: '00000004-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  kind?: WEBSITE,
  serviceId: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** `boolean`

### validateEdgeRules

`services.validateEdgeRules`

Validates an edge ruleset without writing it — returns the same diagnostics updateServiceEdgeRules would reject with (empty list = valid). For live feedback in rule editors

	input: { edgeRules: 'example', environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', serviceId: '00000004-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' },
});
