## Ssh Public Keys

### approveSignup

`sshPublicKeys.approveSignup`

Approve an SSH signup: register the offered SSH key on the authenticated account so the agent is recognized. When the key owns an anonymous trial box, claims that trial into workspaceId and re-owns the key

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.sshPublicKeys.approveSignup({ code: 'postgres' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `code` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `workspaceId` | `string` | No | — |



**Output:** `boolean`


---

### create

`sshPublicKeys.create`

Creates a new SSH public key. When workspaceId is provided (or omitted under a workspace-scoped API token, in which case it defaults to the token's workspace), the key is owned by the workspace and can be used by anyone authenticating as that workspace via native SSH; requires workspace ADMIN access. Otherwise the key is owned by the authenticated user

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.sshPublicKeys.create({
	input: { name: 'api', publicKey: 'example' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `input` | `object` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="input full type">

```ts
{
  name: string,
  publicKey: string,
  workspaceId?: string
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `fingerprint` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `publicKey` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `userId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `workspaceId` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### delete

`sshPublicKeys.delete`

Deletes an SSH public key

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.sshPublicKeys.delete({ id: '00000001-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output:** `boolean`


---

### list

`sshPublicKeys.list`

Gets SSH public keys. If workspaceId is provided, returns the keys owned by that workspace (requires workspace MEMBER access). Under a workspace-scoped API token, workspaceId defaults to the token's workspace when omitted; otherwise returns the authenticated user's personal keys, one page at a time (first/after)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.sshPublicKeys.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `after` | `string` | No | — |
| `before` | `string` | No | — |
| `first` | `number` | No | — |
| `last` | `number` | No | — |
| `workspaceId` | `string` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | The nodes of this page. |
| `pageInfo` | `object \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  createdAt?: string | null,
  fingerprint?: string | null,
  id?: string | null,
  name?: string | null,
  publicKey?: string | null,
  updatedAt?: string | null,
  userId?: string | null,
  workspaceId?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="pageInfo full type">

```ts
{
  hasNextPage?: boolean | null,
  hasPreviousPage?: boolean | null,
  startCursor?: string | null,
  endCursor?: string | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

`sshPublicKeys.signupInfo`

Details for an SSH signup confirm page: the SSH key fingerprint to verify before binding it to the account, and the trial box it owns if any

### updateVolume

`templates.updateVolume`

Sets the default size (in MB) for a volume mount in a template's config. New volumes created when the template is deployed are provisioned at this size, clamped to the deployer's plan maximum. Pass sizeMB: null to clear the pre-size and fall back to the plan default. Editing a template requires maintainer access

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.templates.updateVolume({ serviceId: '00000004-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', templateId: '0000000b-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17', volumeId: '00000007-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `serviceId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `sizeMB` | `number` | No | — |
| `templateId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `volumeId` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `activeProjects` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `canvasConfig` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `category` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `code` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `communityThreadSlug` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |

## Traces

### get

`traces.get`

The spans of one trace, oldest first. Only spans belonging to the environment are returned

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.traces.get({ traceId: '4bf92f3577b34da6a3ce929d0e0e4736', environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `maxSpans` | `number` | No | Spans returned (defaults 1000, max 2000) |
| `traceId` | `string` | Yes | W3C trace id, 32 hex characters |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  component?: string | null,
  deploymentId?: string | null,
  deploymentInstanceId?: string | null,
  durationMs?: number | null,
  events?: {
    attributes?: any | null,
    name?: string | null,
    timestamp?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  kind?: string | null,
  links?: {
    spanId?: string | null,
    traceId?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  name?: string | null,
  parentSpanId?: string | null,
  resourceAttributes?: any | null,
  serviceId?: string | null,
  serviceName?: string | null,
  spanAttributes?: any | null,
  spanId?: string | null,
  startedAt?: string | null,
  statusCode?: string | null,
  statusMessage?: string | null,
  traceId?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### list

`traces.list`

Traces of an environment with at least one span matching the filter, newest first

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.traces.list({ environmentId: '00000003-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `endDate` | `string` | No | Latest span start to consider (ISO); defaults to now |
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `filter` | `string` | No | Filter expression over spans, e.g. @status:error @component:edge @duration:&gt;500 @http.route:/api/users. Built-in fields: trace, span, name, serviceName, service, deployment, replica, component, kind, status, duration (ms). Any other @key looks the key up in the span and resource attributes; free text matches the span name. Prefix a term with - to exclude it. |
| `limit` | `number` | No | Traces returned (defaults 100, max 500) |
| `serviceId` | `string` | No | Only traces with a span from this Railway service (optional) |
| `startDate` | `string` | No | Oldest span start to consider (ISO). Clamped to the project's retention; defaults to one hour ago |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  durationMs?: number | null,
  errorCount?: number | null,
  hasEdge?: boolean | null,
  rootComponent?: string | null,
  rootServerAddress?: string | null,
  rootServiceId?: string | null,
  rootServiceName?: string | null,
  rootSpanName?: string | null,
  rootUrlPath?: string | null,
  serviceName?: string | null,
  spanCount?: number | null,
  startedAt?: string | null,
  traceId?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

`traces.status`

Span activity per service of an environment within retention; a service absent from the list has produced no spans at all


| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `environmentId` | `string` | Yes | — |

```ts
  serviceId?: string | null
}[]
```

## Usage

### estimated

`usage.estimated`

Get the estimated total cost of the project at the end of the current billing cycle. If no `startDate` is provided, the usage for the current billing period of the project owner is returned

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.usage.estimated({
	measurements: ['AGENT_CACHE_READ_TOKENS'],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `includeDeleted` | `boolean` | No | Whether to include deleted projects in estimations. |
| `measurements` | `(AGENT_CACHE_READ_TOKENS \| AGENT_CACHE_WRITE_TOKENS \| AGENT_INPUT_TOKENS \| AGENT_OUTPUT_TOKENS \| AGENT_SPEND_USD \| BACKUP_USAGE_GB \| CPU_LIMIT \| CPU_USAGE \| CPU_USAGE_2 \| DISK_USAGE_GB \| EPHEMERAL_DISK_USAGE_GB \| MEASUREMENT_UNSPECIFIED \| MEMORY_LIMIT_GB \| MEMORY_USAGE_GB \| NETWORK_RX_GB \| NETWORK_TX_GB \| UNRECOGNIZED)[]` | Yes | — |
| `projectId` | `string` | No | — |
| `workspaceId` | `string` | No | — |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  estimatedValue?: number | null,
  measurement?: string | null,
  projectId?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### get

`usage.get`

Get the usage for a single project or all projects for a user/workspace. If no `projectId` or `workspaceId` is provided, the usage for the current user is returned. If no `startDate` is provided, the usage for the current billing period of the project owner is returned

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.usage.get({
	measurements: ['AGENT_CACHE_READ_TOKENS'],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `endDate` | `string` | No | — |
| `groupBy` | `(BILLABLE \| DEPLOYMENT_ID \| DEPLOYMENT_INSTANCE_ID \| ENVIRONMENT_ID \| FEATURE \| HOST_TYPE \| KEY_UNSPECIFIED \| MODEL \| PLUGIN_ID \| PROJECT_ID \| REGION \| SERVICE_ID \| UNRECOGNIZED \| VM_ID \| VOLUME_ID \| VOLUME_INSTANCE_ID \| WORKLOAD_KIND)[]` | No | What to group the aggregated usage by. By default, it is grouped over the entire project. |
| `includeDeleted` | `boolean` | No | Whether to include deleted projects in the usage. |
| `measurements` | `(AGENT_CACHE_READ_TOKENS \| AGENT_CACHE_WRITE_TOKENS \| AGENT_INPUT_TOKENS \| AGENT_OUTPUT_TOKENS \| AGENT_SPEND_USD \| BACKUP_USAGE_GB \| CPU_LIMIT \| CPU_USAGE \| CPU_USAGE_2 \| DISK_USAGE_GB \| EPHEMERAL_DISK_USAGE_GB \| MEASUREMENT_UNSPECIFIED \| MEMORY_LIMIT_GB \| MEMORY_USAGE_GB \| NETWORK_RX_GB \| NETWORK_TX_GB \| UNRECOGNIZED)[]` | Yes | — |
| `projectId` | `string` | No | — |
| `startDate` | `string` | No | — |
| `workspaceId` | `string` | No | — |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  measurement?: string | null,
  tags?: {
    deploymentId?: string | null,
    deploymentInstanceId?: string | null,
    environmentId?: string | null,
    projectId?: string | null,
    region?: string | null,
    serviceId?: string | null,
    volumeId?: string | null,
    volumeInstanceId?: string | null
  } | null,
  value?: number | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

## Volume Instance Backups

### create

`volumeInstanceBackups.create`

Create backup of a volume instance

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.volumeInstanceBackups.create({ volumeInstanceId: '00000008-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `name` | `string` | No | Optional name/label for the backup. Defaults to 'Manual' if not provided. |
| `volumeInstanceId` | `string` | Yes | The id of the volume instance to create a backup of |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `archiveContinuityUnverifiedReason` | `string \| null` | No | Why MySQL archive continuity could not be verified. The restore remains allowed and its image performs the authoritative replay check. |
| `archiveContinuityVerified` | `boolean \| null` | No | For MySQL PITR restores, whether archive continuity was verified before the workflow started. |
| `workflowId` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

| `workflowId` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### restore

`volumeInstanceBackups.restore`

Restore a volume instance from a backup

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.volumeInstanceBackups.restore({ volumeInstanceBackupId: 'example', volumeInstanceId: '00000008-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `replicaServiceIds` | `string[]` | No | Optional: for HA cluster restores, service IDs of replicas that should also receive the backup |
| `volumeInstanceBackupId` | `string` | Yes | The id of the backup to be restored from |
| `volumeInstanceId` | `string` | Yes | The id of the volume instance to be restored from |
| `wipeServiceIds` | `string[]` | No | Service IDs whose volumes should be wiped (replaced with fresh empty volumes) as part of this restore |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `archiveContinuityUnverifiedReason` | `string \| null` | No | Why MySQL archive continuity could not be verified. The restore remains allowed and its image performs the authoritative replay check. |
| `archiveContinuityVerified` | `boolean \| null` | No | For MySQL PITR restores, whether archive continuity was verified before the workflow started. |
| `workflowId` | `string \| null` | No | — |

### pitrRestore

`volumeInstances.pitrRestore`

Point-in-time restore. Creates a brand-new database service in the project, recovered to the target timestamp from the source service's backup history. The source service stays online and untouched

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.volumeInstances.pitrRestore({ targetTimestamp: '2026-04-09T10:00:00Z', volumeInstanceId: '00000008-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `newServiceName` | `string` | No | Optional name for the new restored service. Defaults to '&lt;source&gt;-restored-YYYYMMDD-HHMM'. |
| `sourceRepoPath` | `string` | No | One of the source's backup histories, as listed by the backup history picker. Pass null to use the source's current history. |
| `targetTimestamp` | `string` | Yes | Point-in-time target. Must be within the available restore window. |
| `volumeInstanceId` | `string` | Yes | The id of the volume instance to restore from |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `archiveContinuityUnverifiedReason` | `string \| null` | No | Why MySQL archive continuity could not be verified. The restore remains allowed and its image performs the authoritative replay check. |
| `archiveContinuityVerified` | `boolean \| null` | No | For MySQL PITR restores, whether archive continuity was verified before the workflow started. |
| `workflowId` | `string \| null` | No | — |



---

### pitrRestoreEstimate

`volumeInstances.pitrRestoreEstimate`

Estimated disk space a point-in-time restore to this target needs, and whether it fits your plan's largest volume. Returns null when no estimate is available

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.volumeInstances.pitrRestoreEstimate({ targetTimestamp: '2026-04-09T10:00:00Z', volumeInstanceId: '00000008-5c1e-4d8a-9f43-2b6e1c0a9d17' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `sourceRepoPath` | `string` | No | One of the source's backup histories, as listed by the backup history picker. Pass null to use the source's current history. |
| `targetTimestamp` | `string` | Yes | Point-in-time target the estimate is computed for |
| `volumeInstanceId` | `string` | Yes | The id of the source volume instance to restore from |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `baseBackupLabel` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `dataMB` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `estimatedScratchMB` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `likelyToFit` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `planMaxSizeMB` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `walMB` | `number \| null` | No | — |

## Webhooks

### test

`webhooks.test`

Test a webhook URL by sending a sample payload. Returns the HTTP status code. Custom headers come from `headers`, plus the stored headers of `notificationRuleId` for names not passed

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.webhooks.test({ payload: 'example', url: 'https://example.com/railway/webhook' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `headers` | `object[]` | No | — |
| `notificationRuleId` | `string` | No | — |
| `payload` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `url` | `string` | Yes | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="headers full type">

```ts
{
  name: string,
  value: string
}[]
```

### usageTotals

`workspaces.usageTotals`

Workspace-wide SUM of the same UI usage rollup as `usage` grouped by project. Use this for the usage-page header so it does not wait on per-project rows. Deleted projects that still bill are included when `includeDeleted` is true

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.railway.api.workspaces.usageTotals({
	measurements: ['AGENT_CACHE_READ_TOKENS'],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `endDate` | `string` | No | — |
| `includeDeleted` | `boolean` | No | Whether to include deleted projects in the usage. |
| `measurements` | `(AGENT_CACHE_READ_TOKENS \| AGENT_CACHE_WRITE_TOKENS \| AGENT_INPUT_TOKENS \| AGENT_OUTPUT_TOKENS \| AGENT_SPEND_USD \| BACKUP_USAGE_GB \| CPU_LIMIT \| CPU_USAGE \| CPU_USAGE_2 \| DISK_USAGE_GB \| EPHEMERAL_DISK_USAGE_GB \| MEASUREMENT_UNSPECIFIED \| MEMORY_LIMIT_GB \| MEMORY_USAGE_GB \| NETWORK_RX_GB \| NETWORK_TX_GB \| UNRECOGNIZED)[]` | Yes | — |
| `startDate` | `string` | No | — |
| `workspaceId` | `string` | No | — |



**Output:** `object[]`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
  measurement?: string | null,
  tags?: {
    deploymentId?: string | null,
    deploymentInstanceId?: string | null,
    environmentId?: string | null,
    projectId?: string | null,
    region?: string | null,
    serviceId?: string | null,
    volumeId?: string | null,
    volumeInstanceId?: string | null
  } | null,
  value?: number | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### useInviteCode

`workspaces.useInviteCode`

Use an invite code to join a workspace

**Risk:** `write`

```ts

```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `code` | `string` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `adoptionHistory` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `adoptionLevel` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `apiTokenRateLimit` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `avatar` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `createdAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `customer` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `discordRole` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `has2FAEnforcement` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether 2FA enforcement is enabled for this workspace. |
| `hasAutomaticDiagnosis` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether automatic deployment diagnosis is enabled for this workspace. |
| `hasGuardrailsAccess` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether this workspace has access to guardrails policies. |
| `hasHipaaBAA` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether this workspace has a signed HIPAA Business Associate Agreement, from either the legacy hasBAA flag or the HIPAA_BAA spend commitment feature. |
| `hasSAML` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `id` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `members` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `partnerProfile` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `plan` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `preferredRegion` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `projectCount` | `number \| null` | No | Total number of projects in this workspace. Used by the dashboard to show an exact count without paginating through every project. |
| `redactedDueTo2FAPending` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the current user's access is redacted due to pending 2FA requirement. Returns true if the user is a workspace member, workspace has 2FA enforcement enabled, and the current user needs to enable 2FA. |
| `referredUsers` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `restrictProjectVisibilityToGroups` | `boolean \| null` | No | — |
| `slackChannelId` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `subscriptionPlanLimit` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `updatedAt` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `usersWithout2FA` | `string[] \| null` | No | Get a list of user emails in the workspace who do not have verified 2FA enabled. Returns an empty array if all users have 2FA enabled. |

## Workspace Templates
